We can write correct coordinate descent. For point p, affected shifts m in [max(0,n-1-p), min(2n-2,n-1+p)]. Let s = m-(n-1). The change in c[m] due to d(h[p]):

def cd_fval_vec(c, a, c0, h0, U, lo, hi, vs):
    # c: affected c_s excluding s=0? Or including? We'll pass c_aff excluding diag, a_aff excluding diag, U max unaffected, c0 current diag c_s=0.
    K=len(vs); L=len(c)
    if L>0:
       vals = c[None,:] + vs[:,None]*a[None,:]
       k=vs.max(); out=vals.max(axis=1)
    else: out=np.full(K, U)
    # adjust diagonal: new_c0 = c0 + vs*(1-2h0) - vs**2
    diag = c0 + vs*(1-2h0) - vs*vs
    out=np.maximum(out, diag)
    out=np.maximum(out, U)
    return out* (2/n) maybe no dx? Compare without dx.
```

def fourier_h(n, coeffs): # list of (freq,a,phase)
    x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
    g = np.zeros(n)
    for k,a,phi in coeffs: g += a*np.cos(np.pi*k*x + phi)
    h = 0.5+g
    return project_feasible(clip h)
```

`pair_cd(h,n,max_passes,deadline,pass_step?)`:
```
def pair_cd(h,n, max_passes, deadline, spacing=1, passes_random=0, seed=42, coarse_k=29, fine_k=25, refine=2):
   h=h.copy(); rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   best_cmax = compute_cmax(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
   for pass in range(max_passes):
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      c = compute_c_values(h,n)
      improved=False; changed=0
      # alternating indices to avoid bias: maybe use rng shuffle? For deterministic, use step 1/2 alternating? We'll do sequential but every other pass start offset.
      offset = pass % spacing
      for p in range(offset, n-spacing, spacing):
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          q=p+spacing
          d=pair_line_search(...)
          if abs(d)>1e-14:
             apply; c current; changed+=1; improved=True

def resample_h(h,n, target):
   if len(h)==target: return h.copy()
   x_old=np.linspace(0,2,len(h))
   x_new=np.linspace(0,2,target)
   h_new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,h,period? no)
   return project_feasible(h_new,target)

def pair_fvals(d, c0, cseg, b, dist, L, U, hp, hq, n):
    K=d.size
    if L > 1:
       vals = cseg[None,:] + d[:,None]*b[None,:]
       if 0 < dist < L:
          vals[:, dist-1] += d*d[:,None]
       m = vals.max(axis=1)
    else:
       m = np.full(K, -1e300)
    diag = c0 + 2.0*d*(hq-hp) - 2.0*d*d
    if U > -1e299:
       out = np.maximum(m, diag)
       # max with U scalar
       out = np.maximum(out, U)
    else: return np.maximum(m, diag)
```

    m=c.max()

Detailed functions:
1. `project_feasible(h,n)` as above. Need top-level, no closures.
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h = np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    target = 0.5*n
    for _ in range(200):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12: break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15: break
            h = np.clip(h + diff/tot*room, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15: break
            h = np.clip(h + diff/tot*h, 0.0, 1.0)
    # exact final tiny adjust
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        idxs = np.where((h > 1e-14) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-14))[0]
        attempts = 0

            applied = val - h[i]
            h[i] = val
            diff -= applied
            # recompute idxs? expensive. Instead if not applied enough, move to next interior.
            # Simple: adjust multiple in sequence by filling diff.
        # better distribute over all interior proportionally
    if abs(target - h.sum()) > 1e-12:

        if interior.size:
           # linear solve? just uniform
           step = (target - h.sum()) / interior.size
           h[interior] = np.clip(h[interior] + step, 0,1)
           # repeat once
           diff=target-h.sum()
           for idx in interior:
              if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
              val=np.clip(h[idx]+diff,0,1); h[idx]=val; diff = target-h.sum()? expensive
    return h
```

```
def pair_fvals(d, cseg, b, c0, dist, L, U, hp, hq):
    d = np.asarray(d)
    if L > 1:
        vals = cseg[None, :] + d[:, None] * b[None, :]
        if 0 < dist < L:
            vals[:, dist - 1] += d * d[:, None]
        m = vals.max(axis=1)
    else:
        m = np.full(d.shape[0], -1e300)
    diag = c0 + 2.0 * d * (hq - hp) - 2.0 * d * d
    out = np.maximum(m, diag)
    if U > -1e299:
        out = np.maximum(out, U)
    return out
```

def build_d_grid(lo,hi,coarse_k,near_k):
   if hi-lo <=0: return np.array([0.0])
   grids=[]
   grids.append(np.linspace(lo,hi,coarse_k+1))
   if 0.0 > lo:
      grids.append(np.linspace(lo,0.0,near_k+1))
   if 0.0 < hi:
      grids.append(np.linspace(0.0,hi,near_k+1))
   grids.append(np.array([0.0]))
   return np.concatenate(grids)
```

`pair_cd` with spacing:
```
def pair_cd(h,n,deadline,max_passes=1000,spacing=1,coarse_k=25,near_k=9,refine=2,seed=1):
    h = project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    spans = get_spans(n)
    best_cmax = float(c_values(h,n).max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    pass_no=0
    while max_passes and time.time()<deadline:
       c = c_values(h,n)
       cmax = float(c.max())
       improved = False
       # choose order: if spacing==1 maybe alternate forward/backward
       indices = range(0, n-spacing, spacing)
       if pass_no % 2 == 1:
          # reversed
          idx_list = list(rx) maybe costly. Use step -1 in range? Could just forward.
       for p in idx_list:
           if time.time()>deadline: break
           q=p+spacing
           d,b = pair_line_search(h,c,p,q,spans,cmax,coarse_k,near_k,refine,n)
           if d != 0.0 and b is not None:
               hp=h[p]; hq=h[q]
               apply_pair_update(...)
               # cmax from pair_line? We didn't return f_best. Return f_best too.
               if f_best < cmax - 1e-14: cmax=f_best
               else: cmax=float(c.max())
               improved=True
       if improved and cmax < best_cmax - 1e-14:
           best_cmax=cmax; best_h=h.copy()
       if not improved: break
       pass_no +=1
    # final recompute
    h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    cmax=float(c_values(h,n).max())
    return h, cmax*2/n, best_cmax*2/n
```

```
def random_pair_round(h,n,max_pairs,deadline,seed,coarse_k=21,near_k=6,refine=1):
   h=project_feasible(h,n).copy(); spans=get_spans(n)
   c=c_values(h,n); cmax=float(c.max()); best=...; improved_round=False
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   for i in range(max_pairs):
      if time>deadline: break
      p=rng.randint(n); q=rng.randint(n)
      if p==q: continue
      ok,fnew=try_pair_update(h,c,p,q,spans,cmax,coarse_k,near_k,refine,n)
      if ok:
          cmax=fnew; best_h=h.copy(); best_cmax=cmax; improved_round=True
          if i%50==0: cmax=float(c.max()) # sync
   return h,best...
```

`perturb_h`:
```
def perturb_h(h,n,seed,scale,block=False):
   h=h.copy(); rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   if block:
      # random local complement pairs preserve sum
      k=min(20,n//5)
      for _ in range(k):
         i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n); if i!=j:
             # swap values
             h[i],h[j]=h[j],h[i]
   else:
      h += scale*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)
   return project_feasible(h,n)
```
Actually swapping preserves sum; project no change. Complement block pairs: complementing two not preserve sum unless values avg 0.5? Swapping better.

But if we swap values, objective may increase; we'll polish and keep best.

`try_single_update`:
```
def try_single_update(h,c,p,spans,curr,coarse_k,near_k,refine,n):
    h0=h[p]; lo=-h0; hi=1-h0
    L=int(spans[p])
    c0=c[0]
    if L>1:
       a=np.zeros(L-1)
       a_len=min(L-1,n-1-p); if ... a[:a_len]+=1-h[p+1:...]; a_len=min(L-1,p); if... a[:a_len]-=h[p-1:...:-1]
       cseg=c[1:L]
    else ...
    U = max(c[L:])
    grid d; fvals_single(d,cseg,a,c0,L,U,h0)
    refine; if improve apply h[p]=h0+d, c[1:L]+=d*a, c[0]+=d*(1-2h0)-d*d; return True,fbest
```

Need `resample_h` if target n not same:
```
def resample_h(h, n_old, n_new):
   if n_old == n_new: return h.copy()
   xo=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_old)
   xn=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_new)
   return project_feasible(np.interp(xn,xo,h), n_new)
```
Note np.interp endpoints; sum after project.

Perturb:
```
def perturb_h(h,n,seed,scale):
    h=h.copy(); rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h += scale * rng.normal(0,1,n)
    # random swaps to preserve more
    for _ in range(max(3, n//100)):
       i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
       if i!=j: h[i],h[j]=h[j],h[i]
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```
No lambdas.

Need maybe incorporate `initial_h_values` if not available? The problem says n=700 samples. If not, create random Fourier to not constant:
```
def make_random_h(n,seed):
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
   g=np.zeros(n)
   for k in range(1,9):
       g += 0.08*np.exp(-0.25*(k-1))*rng.uniform(-1,1)*np.cos(np.pi*k*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
   return project_feasible(np.clip(0.5+g,0,1),n)
```
But if initial available, use.

For q subtract:
- q first: - (1 - h[q+s]) for s=1..n-1-q: slice q+1...
- q second: -(-h[q-s]) = +h[q-s]: slice q-1...

Let's code `try_pair_update` carefully:
```
def try_pair_update(h, c, p, q, spans, curr, coarse_k, near_k, refine, n):
    hp = float(h[p]); hq = float(h[q])
    lo = max(-hp, hq - 1.0)
    hi = min(1.0 - hp, hq)
    if hi - lo <= 1e-14:
        return False, None, curr
    L = int(max(spans[p], spans[q]))
    c0 = float(c[0])
    if L > 1:
        b = np.zeros(L - 1)
        a = min(L - 1, n - 1 - p)
        if a > 0:
            b[:a] += 1.0 - h[p + 1:p + 1 + a]
        a = min(L - 1, p)
        if a > 0:
            b[:a] -= h[p - 1:p - 1 - a:-1]
        a = min(L - 1, n - 1 - q)
        if a > 0:
            b[:a] -= 1.0 - h[q + 1:q + 1 + a]
        a = min(L - 1, q)
        if a > 0:
            b[:a] += h[q - 1:q - 1 - a:-1]
        cseg = c[1:L]
    else:
        b = np.zeros(0)
        cseg = np.zeros(0)
    dist = abs(p - q)
    if L < n:
        U = float(c[L:].max()) if L < n else -1e300  # but L<n check
    else:
        U = -1e300
    d = build_d_grid(lo, hi, coarse_k, near_k)
    f = eval_pair(d, cseg, b, c0, dist, L, U, hp, hq)
    imin = int(np.argmin(f))
    fbest = float(f[imin]); dbest = float(d[imin])
    if 0.0 < imin < len(d) - 1: # but d unsorted due concatenate? argmin index not meaningful for interior!
```
Ah! build_d_grid concatenates base and near0; d not sorted, so index interior not meaningful. Need determine if dbest is near boundaries, not index. Use `if dbest > lo + 1e-12 and dbest < hi - 1e-12:`. Good.

    step = max((hi - lo) / max(coarse_k,1), 1e-6)
    half = step
    for _ in range(refine):

        if r - l < 1e-14: break
        df = build_refine_grid(l,r,dbest,coarse? maybe fine_k=21)
        ff = eval_pair(df,...)
        imin2=argmin; fb2=float(ff[imin2]); db2=float(df[imin2])
        if fb2 < fbest - 1e-13:
            fbest=fb2; dbest=db2
            if l + 1e-12 < dbest < r - 1e-12:
                half *= 0.45
            else:
                half *= 0.8
        else:
            break
```

```
def build_refine_grid(l,r,center,k):
    arr = np.linspace(l,r,k+1)
    if l < center < r: arr = np.concatenate((arr,[center]))
    return arr
```

`build_d_grid`: Use `np.array([0.0])`. Ensure 0 within [lo,hi]; it is. If hi very small? 0 okay.
```
def build_d_grid(lo, hi, coarse_k, near_k):
    lo=float(lo); hi=float(hi)
    if hi <= lo:
       return np.array([0.0])
    parts=[np.linspace(lo, hi, max(3, coarse_k))]
    if lo < 0.0:
       parts.append(np.linspace(lo, 0.0, max(2, near_k)))
    if hi > 0.0:
       parts.append(np.linspace(0.0, hi, max(2, near_k)))
    parts.append(np.array([0.0]))
    return np.concatenate(parts)
```
Duplicates many but K manageable. Could remove duplicates? not needed. But argmin with duplicates okay. For refinement interior, use dbest.
Maybe near grid includes 0 twice, okay.

`eval_pair`:
```
def eval_pair(d, cseg, b, c0, dist, L, U, hp, hq):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=float)
    K=d.size
    if L > 1:
       vals = cseg[None, :] + d[:, None] * b[None, :]
       if 0 < dist < L:
          vals[:, dist-1] += d * d[:, None]
       m = vals.max(axis=1)
    else:
       m = np.full(K, -1e300)
    diag = c0 + 2.0*d*(hq-hp) - 2.0*d*d
    out = np.maximum(m, diag)
    if U > -1e299:
       out = np.maximum(out, U)
    return out
```
If cseg/b empty but L>1? L>1 => length >=1. ok.

`pair_cd`:
```
def pair_cd(h,n,deadline,spacing=1,coarse_k=27,near_k=9,refine=2,seed=1,max_passes=1000):
    h = project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    spans = get_spans(n)
    c = c_values(h,n)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    best_cmax = cmax
    best_h = h.copy()
    pass_i=0
    improved_any=False
    while pass_i < max_passes and time.time() < deadline:
        c = c_values(h,n)
        cmax = float(c.max())
        improved=False
        # choose direction
        if pass_i % 2 == 0:
            pairs = range(0, n-spacing, spacing)
        else:
            pairs = range(n-spacing-1, -1, -1)  # if step -1? Not with spacing? Could use range(n-spacing-1,-1,-spacing)
        for p in pairs:
            if time.time() >= deadline: break
            q = p + spacing
            if q >= n: continue
            ok, d, fnew = try_pair_update(h,c,p,q,spans,cmax,coarse_k,near_k,refine,n)
            if ok:
                cmax = fnew
                improved=True
                # periodic sync
        if improved:
            improved_any=True
            # recompute exact cmax maybe
            cmax = float(c_values(h,n).max()) # maybe expensive but once per pass ok
            if cmax < best_cmax - 1e-12:
                best_cmax = cmax
                best_h = h.copy()
        else:
            break
        pass_i += 1
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h,n)
    cmax = float(c_values(best_h,n).max())
    return best_h, cmax * (2.0/n), improved_any or (cmax < best_cmax?)
```
But pass_i direction reverse: `range(n-spacing-1, -1, -spacing)` works for spacing 2? Gives p descending by spacing from last valid. Good.
However pairs with spacing p,p+spacing; reverse covers maybe different parity sets? For spacing=1 covers all. For spacing=2, forward pairs (0,2),(1,3),...; reverse is (n-spacing-1,n-spacing), etc, same parity sets. Good.
We should maybe randomize pair order to avoid bias; use rng permutation per pass? Cost O(n log n), okay but maybe not. We can use rng to shuffle p values with spacing? If we shuffle, pairs independent. But need ensure p+spacing<n. Could do:
```
rng = RandomState(seed+pass_i); order=list(range(0,n-spacing,spacing)); rng.shuffle(order); for p in order
```

def random_pair_round(h,n,deadline,max_pairs,seed,coarse_k=21,near_k=6,refine=1,spacing=0):
   h=project_feasible(h,n).copy(); spans=get_spans(n); rng=RandomState(seed)
   c=c_values(h,n); cmax=float(c.max()); best_cmax=cmax; best_h=h.copy()
   for i in range(max_pairs):
      if time.time()>=deadline: break
      if spacing>0: p=rng.randint(0,n-spacing); q=p+spacing
      else: p=int(rng.randint(n)); q=int(rng.randint(n)); if p==q: continue
      ok,d,fnew=try_pair_update(...)
      if ok:
         cmax=fnew
         if i%25==0: cmax=float(c.max()) # c max O(n), not correlate
         if cmax < best_cmax - 1e-12:
             best_cmax=cmax; best_h=h.copy()
   # final recompute
   best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n); cmax=float(c_values(best_h,n).max())
   return best_h,cmax*2/n,cmax<best_cmax? Need before final? Just return improved any.
```

`make_random_h` fallback:
```
def make_random_h(n, seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    g=np.zeros(n)
    for k in range(1,9):
       amp=0.12/(1+0.5*(k-1))
       phi=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       g += amp*np.cos(np.pi*k*x + phi)
    return project_feasible(np.clip(0.5+g,0,1),n)
```
Average modes ensure mean near 0.5? Cos over length 2 integral zero for integer k, but endpoints discrete may not exactly; project. Good.

```
def compute_AB(h,n):
    A=np.empty(n); B=np.empty(n)
    c0 = float(np.sum(h*(1-h)))
    A[0]=B[0]=c0
    for r in range(1,n):
       m=n-r
       A[r]=float(np.dot(h[:m], 1-h[r:r+m])) # or sum
       B[r]=float(np.dot(h[r:r+m], 1-h[:m]))
    return A,B
```

`pair_cd` with A/B:
```
def pair_cd(h,n,deadline,spacing=1,coarse_k=27,near_k=9,refine=2,seed=1,max_passes=1000):
    h=project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    A,B=compute_AB(h,n)
    cmax=float(max(A.max(),B.max()))
    best_cmax=cmax; best_h=h.copy(); improved_any=False
    spans=get_spans(n)
    pass_i=0
    while ...:
       A,B=compute_AB(h,n); cmax=max...
       improved=False
       order=np.arange(0,n-spacing,spacing)
       rng=np.random.RandomState(seed+pass_i); rng.shuffle(order)
       for p in order:
          if time>deadline: break
          q=p+spacing
          ok,d,fnew=try_pair_update(h,A,B,p,q,spans,cmax,...)
          if ok:
             cmax=fnew; improved=True
             if (impaired_count%25)==0: cmax=max(A.max(),B.max())
       if improved:
          improved_any=True
          A,B=compute_AB(h,n); cmax=max...
          if cmax < best_cmax - 1e-12:
             best_cmax=cmax; best_h=h.copy()
       else: break
       pass_i++
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    c5=compute_c5_full(best_h,n) # expensive but once
    return best_h,c5,improved_any
```
Need if pass_i gets huge, seed+pass_i ok. `np.arange(0,n-spacing,spacing)` if spacing>=n empty. For spacing > n, skip.
If no pairs, improved false.

Can we compute A/B using `np.correlate` faster? Let's derive to reduce Python loop. A_r = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-r} h_i(1-h_{i+r}). The product term P_r = ∑ h_i h_{i+r} for r≥0. This is autocorrelation of h at lag r. P_0=sum(h²). P_r for r=1..n-1 can be computed via `np.correlate(h,h,mode='full')[n-1:]`? Numpy correlate h,h high half? For a=h,v=h, output index n-1+r = ∑ a[i+r]v[i] = ∑ h_{i+r}h_i = P_r. Good. So product array P for r=0..n-1 = correlate(h,h,'full')[n-1:]. Then sumS_r? A_r = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-r} h_i - P_r. Let prefix sums of h: H_r = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-r} h_i. B_r = ∑_{i=r}^{n-1} h_i - P_r. Since B sum over i+r = r..n-1. Let H_start_r = sum h[r:n]. Then A=H_end_r - P_r where H_end_r=sum h[0:n-r]; B=H_start_r - P_r. This can be vectorized! Great.
Let's verify: A_r = ∑ h_i(1-h_{i+r}) = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-r} h_i - P_r. B_r = ∑ h_{i+r}(1-h_i) = ∑_{i=r}^{n-1} h_i - P_r. Yes.
So compute:
```
def compute_AB(h,n):
    pref = np.concatenate(([0], np.cumsum(h)))  # length n+1
    H_end = pref[n-r] for r=0..n-1? sum h[0:n-r] = pref[n-r]
    H_start = pref[n] - pref[r]
    P = np.correlate(h,h,mode='full')[n-1:]  # length n, P[0]=sum h², P[r]=∑h_i h_{i+r}
    A = H_end - P
    B = H_start - P
    return A,B
```
Need H_end array: for r=0, pref[n]; r=1 pref[n-1]; ... r=n-1 pref[1]. So `H_end = pref[1:n][::-1]?` Let's make:
```
r=np.arange(n)
H_end = pref[n - r]  # length n, for r=0 pref[n], r=n-1 pref[1]. Works with array indexing.
H_start = pref[n] - pref[r]
P = np.correlate(h, h, mode='full')[n-1:]
A = H_end - P
B = H_start - P
```
This is O(n²) for correlate but C, faster and no Python loop. Excellent.
Check P from correlate(h,h). For n=2 h=[a,b], output high [a²+ab? wait correlate full: [a*b, a²+b², b*a]. high index n-1=1 => a²+b² = P0. index2 => b*a=P1. Good.
A1 = sum h[0:n-1]=a - P1=ab = a(1-b). B1 = sum h[1:]=b - ab = b(1-a). Good.
Thus compute_AB vectorized. Great.

          idx=dist-1
          vals[:, idx] += dd
          vals[:, idx+half_len] += dd
      m=vals.max(axis=1)
   else: m=np.full(K,-1e300)
   diag=...

```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 10.0
    try:
       h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
       n = len(h)
    except Exception:
       n = 700
       h = make_random_h(n, seed)
    if n < 4:
       n=700; h=make_random_h(n,seed)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    A,B = compute_AB(h,n)
    best_c = float(max(A.max(), B.max()) * (2.0/n))
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_n = n
    any_improve = True
    # Maybe if best_c > 0.38, construct random alternatives? but initial good.
    # Main local on n0:
    main_end = min(deadline, t0 + 620.0)
    cyc_seed = seed
    while time.time() < main_end:
        cyc_improved = False
        for spacing in (1, 2, 3, 1, 4, 6):
            if time.time() >= main_end: break
            end = min(main_end, time.time() + 70.0)
            if n - spacing < 1: continue
            h2,c2,imp = pair_cd(best_h, n, end, spacing=spacing, coarse_k=27, near_k=9, refine=2, seed=cyc_seed)
            cyc_seed += 1
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_h = h2.copy(); best_c = c2; cyc_improved = True
        if time.time() >= main_end: break
        end = min(main_end, time.time() + 45.0)
        h2,c2,imp = random_pair_round(best_h, n, end, 2500, seed=cyc_seed, spacing=0, coarse_k=19, near_k=6, refine=1)
        cyc_seed += 100
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_h = h2.copy(); best_c = c2; cyc_improved = True
        if not cyc_improved:
            break
    # Resampling n sweep
    n_candidates = [best_n, 500, 650, 800, 1000, 450, 550, 750, 900]
    # maybe sort by distance to best_n? Use set to avoid repeats.
    seen=set()
    for nt in n_candidates:
       if time.time() >= deadline - 60: break
       if nt in seen or nt < 10 or nt > 1400: continue
       seen.add(nt)
       if nt == best_n:
           h_try = best_h.copy()
       else:
           h_try = resample_h(best_h, best_n, nt)
       end = min(deadline - 50, time.time() + 65.0)
       if end <= time.time(): continue
       h2,c2,imp = pair_cd(h_try, nt, end, spacing=1, coarse_k=25, near_k=8, refine=2, seed=cyc_seed)
       cyc_seed += 5
       if c2 < best_c - 1e-12 or (abs(c2-best_c)<=1e-12 and nt < best_n):
           best_h=h2.copy(); best_c=c2; best_n=nt
       if time.time() < deadline - 60:
           end = min(deadline - 60, time.time() + 35.0)
           h2,c2,imp = random_pair_round(best_h, nt, end, 1200, seed=cyc_seed, spacing=0, coarse_k=17, near_k=5, refine=1)
           cyc_seed += 5
           if c2 < best_c - 1e-12 or (abs(c2-best_c)<=1e-12 and nt < best_n):
               best_h=h2.copy(); best_c=c2; best_n=nt
       # If best_n changed, break maybe to revisit? We can continue.
    # Final perturb restarts
    restart_seed = cyc_seed
    while time.time() < deadline - 25.0:
        scale = 0.01 + 0.04 * (0.5 + 0.5*np.sin(restart_seed))  # deterministic
        h_try = perturb_h(best_h, best_n, restart_seed, scale)
        end = min(deadline - 20.0, time.time() + 40.0)
        if end <= time.time()+5: break
        h2,c2,imp = pair_cd(h_try, best_n, end, spacing=1, coarse_k=23, near_k=8, refine=1, seed=restart_seed)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_h=h2.copy(); best_c=c2
        else:
            # maybe random round on current best if time
            # no, continue
            pass
        restart_seed += 1
        if restart_seed % 4 == 0 and time.time() < deadline - 30:
            end=min(deadline-25, time.time()+25)
            h2,c2,imp=random_pair_round(best_h,best_n,end,800,seed=restart_seed,spacing=0,coarse_k=17,near_k=5,refine=1)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_h=h2.copy(); best_c=c2
    # Final polish on best
    if time.time() < deadline - 5.0:
       end=deadline-2.0
       h2,c2,imp=pair_cd(best_h,best_n,end,spacing=1,coarse_k=29,near_k=10,refine=2,seed=restart_seed)
       if c2 < best_c - 1e-12: best_h=...
       # maybe spacing 2
       if time.time() < deadline - 2:
          h2,c2,imp=pair_cd(best_h,best_n,deadline-0.5,spacing=2,coarse_k=25,near_k=8,refine=1,seed=restart_seed+7)
          if c2 < best_c - 1e-12: best...
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    c_final = compute_c5_full(best_h,best_n)
    return best_h, float(c_final), best_n
```

Now, optimize project for speed:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy(); h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    target=0.5*n
    for _ in range(200):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
          if tot<=1e-15: break
          h=np.clip(h + diff/tot*room,0,1)
       else:
          tot=h.sum()
          if tot<=1e-15: break
          h=np.clip(h + diff/tot*h,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       idx=np.where((h>1e-14)&(h<1-1e-14))[0]
       if idx.size:
          # distribute uniformly then residual sequential
          step=diff/idx.size
          h[idx]=np.clip(h[idx]+step,0,1)
          diff=target-h.sum()
          if abs(diff)>1e-14:
             for i in idx:
                if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
                v=np.clip(h[i]+diff,0,1)
                applied=v-h[i]; h[i]=v; diff -= applied
    return h
```
If step pushes bounds, residual distributed. Good.

def build_refine_grid(lo, hi, center, k):
    lo=float(lo); hi=float(hi); center=float(center)
    if hi <= lo: return np.array([center])
    arr = np.linspace(lo, hi, int(max(3, k)))
    if lo < center < hi:
       arr = np.concatenate((arr, np.array([center])))

```
import numpy as np
import time

def project_feasible(h,n): ...
def get_spans(n):
    a=np.arange(n,dtype=int)
    return np.maximum(a, n-1-a)+1
def compute_AB(h,n):
    pref=np.empty(n+1,float); pref[0]=0; pref[1:]=np.cumsum(h)
    r=np.arange(n)
    H_end=pref[n-r]
    H_start=pref[n]-pref[r]
    P=np.correlate(h,h,mode='full')[n-1:]
    A=H_end-P
    B=H_start-P
    A=np.maximum(A,0.0)
    B=np.maximum(B,0.0)
    return A,B
def c5_from_ab(A,B,n):
    m=max(float(A.max()), float(B.max()))
    return m*(2.0/n)
def c5_full(h,n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full'))*(2.0/n))
def make_random_h(n,seed): ...
def resample_h(h,n_old,n_new): ...
def perturb_h(h,n,seed,scale): ...
def build_d_grid(lo,hi,coarse_k,near_k): ...
def build_refine_grid(lo,hi,center,k): ...
def eval_pair(d,v0,bv,c0,half_len,dist,L,U,hp,hq): ...
def try_pair_update(...): ...
def pair_cd(...): ...
def random_pair_round(...): ...
def run(...): ...
```
No closures/lambdas.

Check `pref[n-r]`: r array int, n-r array. For r=0, pref[n]; r=n-1, pref[1]. Good. `pref[r]` r=0..n-1. `H_start=pref[n]-pref[r]`: sum h[r:n]. For r=n-1, h[n-1]. Good.

`np.correlate(h,h,'full')` may be slow for n=1? if n=1, high P length1. A = pref[1]-P = h0 - h0² = h0(1-h0); B same. Good.

Now, code for try_pair_update in detail:
```
def try_pair_update(h, A, B, p, q, spans, curr, coarse_k, near_k, refine, n):
    hp = float(h[p]); hq = float(h[q])
    lo = max(-hp, hq - 1.0)
    hi = min(1.0 - hp, hq)
    if hi - lo <= 1e-13:
        return False, 0.0, curr
    L = int(max(spans[p], spans[q]))
    c0 = float(A[0])
    half = L - 1
    if half > 0:
        bA = np.zeros(half)
        bB = np.zeros(half)
        a = min(half, n - 1 - p)
        if a > 0:
            bA[:a] += 1.0 - h[p+1:p+1+a]
        a = min(half, p)
        if a > 0:
            bA[:a] -= h[p-1:p-1-a:-1]
        a = min(half, p)
        if a > 0:
            bB[:a] += 1.0 - h[p-1:p-1-a:-1]
        a = min(half, n - 1 - p)
        if a > 0:
            bB[:a] -= h[p+1:p+1+a]
        a = min(half, n - 1 - q)
        if a > 0:
            bA[:a] -= 1.0 - h[q+1:q+1+a]
        a = min(half, q)
        if a > 0:
            bA[:a] += h[q-1:q-1-a:-1]
        a = min(half, q)
        if a > 0:
            bB[:a] -= 1.0 - h[q-1:q-1-a:-1]
        a = min(half, n - 1 - q)
        if a > 0:
            bB[:a] += h[q+1:q+1+a]
        Aseg = A[1:L]
        Bseg = B[1:L]
        v0 = np.concatenate((Aseg, Bseg))
        bv = np.concatenate((bA, bB))
    else:
        bA = bB = np.zeros(0)
        v0 = np.zeros(0)
        bv = np.zeros(0)
    dist = abs(p - q)
    if L < n:
        Ua = float(A[L:].max()) if L < n else -1e300
        Ub = float(B[L:].max()) if L < n else -1e300
        U = max(Ua, Ub)
    else:
        U = -1e300
    d = build_d_grid(lo, hi, coarse_k, near_k)
    f = eval_pair(d, v0, bv, c0, half, dist, L, U, hp, hq)
    imin = int(np.argmin(f))
    fbest = float(f[imin]); dbest = float(d[imin])
    # refine
    if refine > 0 and dbest > lo + 1e-12 and dbest < hi - 1e-12:
        step = max((hi - lo) / max(1, coarse_k), 1e-6)
        halfw = step
        for _ in range(refine):
            l = max(lo, dbest - halfw)
            r = min(hi, dbest + halfw)
            if r - l < 1e-14:
                break
            df = build_refine_grid(l, r, dbest, 23)
            ff = eval_pair(df, v0, bv, c0, half, dist, L, U, hp, hq)
            imin2 = int(np.argmin(ff))
            fb2 = float(ff[imin2]); db2 = float(df[imin2])
            if fb2 < fbest - 1e-12:
                fbest = fb2; dbest = db2
                if l + 1e-12 < dbest < r - 1e-12:
                    halfw *= 0.45
                else:
                    halfw *= 0.75
            else:
                break
    # final eval at dbest and 0 maybe
    if abs(dbest) < 1e-13:
        return False, 0.0, curr
    fcheck = eval_pair(np.array([0.0, dbest]), v0, bv, c0, half, dist, L, U, hp, hq)
    if fcheck[1] < curr - 1e-8 and fcheck[1] <= fcheck[0] + 1e-12:
        # apply
        newp = float(np.clip(hp + dbest, 0.0, 1.0))
        newq = float(np.clip(hq - dbest, 0.0, 1.0))
        h[p] = newp; h[q] = newq
        if half > 0:
            A[1:L] += dbest * bA
            B[1:L] += dbest * bB
            if 0 < dist < L:
                A[dist] += dbest * dbest
                B[dist] += dbest * dbest
        newc0 = c0 + 2.0 * dbest * (hq - hp) - 2.0 * dbest * dbest
        if newc0 < 0.0:
            newc0 = 0.0
        A[0] = newc0; B[0] = newc0
        return True, dbest, float(fcheck[1])
    return False, 0.0, curr
```

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(300):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * room, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = float(h.sum())
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        idx = np.where((h > 1e-14) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-14))[0]
        if idx.size > 0:
            step = diff / idx.size
            h[idx] = np.clip(h[idx] + step, 0.0, 1.0)
            diff = target - float(h.sum())
            if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
                for i in idx:
                    if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
                        break
                    v = float(np.clip(h[i] + diff, 0.0, 1.0))
                    h[i] = v
                    diff = target - float(h[i]? Wait after changing h[i], recompute full sum expensive inside loop. We can track applied.
```
Better final residual:
```
            for i in idx:
                if abs(diff) < 1e-14: break
                old = h[i]
                v = np.clip(old + diff, 0,1)
                applied = v-old
                h[i]=v
                diff -= applied
```
This uses accumulated diff, no recompute. If applied less due to clip, continue to next. Good.

    return h
```

`make_random_h`:
```
def make_random_h(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for k in range(1, 9):
        amp = 0.20 / (1.0 + 0.4 * (k - 1))
        phi = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        g += amp * np.cos(np.pi * k * x + phi)
    return project_feasible(np.clip(0.5 + g, 0.0, 1.0), n)
```
Means not exact but project.

`resample_h`:
```
def resample_h(h, n_old, n_new):
    if n_old == n_new: return h.copy()
    xo = np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_old)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_new)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(xn,xo,h), n_new)
```

`perturb_h`:
```
def perturb_h(h,n,seed,scale):
    h = h.copy()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h += scale * rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)
    swaps = max(3, n // 100)
    for _ in range(swaps):
        i = int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
        if i != j:
            h[i],h[j] = h[j],h[i]
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```

`build_d_grid`: If coarse_k maybe int. Ensure lo<hi. Use parts list.
```
def build_d_grid(lo, hi, coarse_k, near_k):
    lo=float(lo); hi=float(hi)
    if hi <= lo: return np.array([0.0])
    parts = [np.linspace(lo, hi, int(max(3, coarse_k)))]
    if lo < 0.0:
       parts.append(np.linspace(lo,0.0,int(max(2,near_k))))
    if hi > 0.0:
       parts.append(np.linspace(0.0,hi,int(max(2,near_k))))
    parts.append(np.array([0.0]))
    return np.concatenate(parts)
```

`build_refine_grid`: similar.

`eval_pair`: Need handle `d` size 0? not.
```
def eval_pair(d,v0,bv,c0,half_len,dist,L,U,hp,hq):
    d=np.asarray(d,dtype=float)
    K=d.size
    if half_len > 0:
        vals = v0[None,:] + d[:,None] * bv[None,:]
        if 0 < dist < L:
            dd = d * d[:,None]
            idx = dist - 1
            vals[:, idx] += dd
            vals[:, idx + half_len] += dd
        m = vals.max(axis=1)
    else:
        m = np.full(K, -1e300)
    diag = c0 + 2.0*d*(hq-hp) - 2.0*d*d
    out = np.maximum(m, diag)
    if U > -1e299:
        out = np.maximum(out, U)
    return out
```
If L=1, half_len=0, dist maybe >0? no if n>1 L>1.

Now, `pair_cd`:
```
def pair_cd(h,n,deadline,spacing=1,coarse_k=27,near_k=9,refine=2,seed=1,max_passes=10000):
    h = project_feasible(h, n).copy()
    A,B = compute_AB(h,n)
    cmax = max(float(A.max()), float(B.max()))
    best_cmax = cmax
    best_h = h.copy()
    spans = get_spans(n)
    improved_any=False
    pass_i=0
    while pass_i < max_passes and time.time() < deadline:
        A,B = compute_AB(h,n)
        cmax = max(float(A.max()), float(B.max()))
        improved=False
        if spacing >= n:
            break
        order = np.arange(0, n - spacing, spacing, dtype=int)
        if order.size:
            rng = np.random.RandomState(seed + pass_i)
            rng.shuffle(order)
        upd_count=0
        for p in order:
            if time.time() >= deadline: break
            q = p + spacing
            ok, d, fnew = try_pair_update(h,A,B,p,q,spans,cmax,coarse_k,near_k,refine,n)
            if ok:
                cmax = fnew
                updated = True
                upd_count += 1
                if upd_count % 25 == 0:
                    cmax = max(float(A.max()), float(B.max()))
        if improved:
            improved_any=True
            A,B = compute_AB(h,n)
            cmax = max(float(A.max()), float(B.max()))
            if cmax < best_cmax - 1e-12:
                best_cmax = cmax
                best_h = h.copy()
        else:
            break
        pass_i += 1
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    A,B = compute_AB(best_h,n)
    c5 = max(float(A.max()), float(B.max())) * (2.0 / n)
    return best_h, c5, improved_any
```
Need `improved` assigned in loop: In code above after ok, set `improved=True`. Ensure variable initialized. Good.

If `deadline` in past, while skip, final recompute initial. Good.

Potential compact `project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,float).copy(),0,1)
    t=0.5*n
    for _ in range(300):
        d=t-h.sum()
        if abs(d)<1e-13: break
        if d>0:
            r=1-h; s=r.sum()
            if s<=1e-15: break
            h=np.clip(h+d/s*r,0,1)
        else:
            s=h.sum()
            if s<=1e-15: break
            h=np.clip(h+d/s*h,0,1)
    d=t-h.sum()
    if abs(d)>1e-13:
        idx=np.where((h>1e-14)&(h<1-1e-14))[0]
        if idx.size:
            h[idx]+=d/idx.size
            h=np.clip(h,0,1)
            d=t-h.sum()
            for i in idx:
                if abs(d)<1e-14: break
                v=np.clip(h[i]+d,0,1); d-=v-h[i]; h[i]=v
    return h
```
~18 lines.

`compute_AB` ~10.

`build_d_grid` compact:
```
def build_d_grid(lo,hi,a,b):
    lo,hi=float(lo),float(hi)
    if hi<=lo: return np.array([0.])
    p=[np.linspace(lo,hi,int(max(3,a)))]
    if lo<0: p.append(np.linspace(lo,0,int(max(2,b))))
    if hi>0: p.append(np.linspace(0,hi,int(max(2,b))))
    for e in (-5,-4,-3,-2,-1):
        if hi>0: p.append([hi*10.0**e])
        if lo<0: p.append([lo*10.0**e])
    p.append([0.])
    return np.concatenate([arr if isinstance(arr,np.ndarray) else np.array(arr) for arr in p])
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(300):
        d = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(d) < 1e-13:
            break
        if d > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            s = float(room.sum())
            if s <= 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (d / s) * room, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            s = float(h.sum())
            if s <= 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (d / s) * h, 0.0, 1.0)
    d = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(d) > 1e-13:
        idx = np.where((h > 1e-14) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-14))[0]
        if idx.size:
            h[idx] += d / idx.size
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
            d = target - float(h.sum())
            for i in idx:
                if abs(d) < 1e-14:
                    break
                v = float(np.clip(h[i] + d, 0.0, 1.0))
                d = d - (v - h[i])
                h[i] = v
    return h


def get_spans(n):
    a = np.arange(n, dtype=int)
    return np.maximum(a, n - 1 - a) + 1


def compute_ab(h, n):
    pref = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
    pref[0] = 0.0
    pref[1:] = np.cumsum(h)
    r = np.arange(n)
    left = pref[n - r]
    right = pref[n] - pref[r]
    prod = np.correlate(h, h, mode='full')[n - 1:]
    a = np.maximum(left - prod, 0.0)
    b = np.maximum(right - prod, 0.0)
    return a, b


def c5_full(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * (2.0 / n))


def make_random_h(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for k in range(1, 9):
        amp = 0.20 / (1.0 + 0.4 * (k - 1))
        phi = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        g += amp * np.cos(np.pi * k * x + phi)
    return project_feasible(np.clip(0.5 + g, 0.0, 1.0), n)


def resample_h(h, n_old, n_new):
    if n_old == n_new:
        return h.copy()
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(xn, xo, h), n_new)


def perturb_h(h, n, seed, scale):
    h = h.copy()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h += scale * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    swaps = max(3, n // 100)
    for _ in range(swaps):
        i = int(rng.randint(n))
        j = int(rng.randint(n))
        if i != j:
            h[i], h[j] = h[j], h[i]
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def build_d_grid(lo, hi, coarse_k, near_k):
    lo = float(lo)
    hi = float(hi)
    if hi <= lo:
        return np.array([0.0])
    parts = [np.linspace(lo, hi, int(max(3, coarse_k)))]
    if lo < 0.0:
        parts.append(np.linspace(lo, 0.0, int(max(2, near_k))))
    if hi > 0.0:
        parts.append(np.linspace(0.0, hi, int(max(2, near_k))))
    small = []
    for e in (-5.0, -4.0, -3.0, -2.0, -1.0):
        if hi > 0.0:
            small.append(hi * (10.0 ** e))
        if lo < 0.0:
            small.append(lo * (10.0 ** e))
    if small:
        parts.append(np.array(small, dtype=float))
    parts.append(np.array([0.0]))
    return np.concatenate(parts)


def build_refine_grid(lo, hi, center, k):
    lo = float(lo)
    hi = float(hi)
    center = float(center)
    if hi <= lo:
        return np.array([center])
    arr = np.linspace(lo, hi, int(max(3, k)))
    if lo < center < hi:
        arr = np.concatenate((arr, np.array([center])))
    return arr


def eval_pair(d, v0, bv, c0, half_len, dist, L, U, hp, hq):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=float)
    ks = d.size
    if half_len > 0:
        vals = v0[None, :] + d[:, None] * bv[None, :]
        if 0 < dist < L:
            dd = d * d[:, None]
            idx = dist - 1
            vals[:, idx] += dd
            vals[:, idx + half_len] += dd
        m = vals.max(axis=1)
    else:
        m = np.full(ks, -1e300)
    diag = c0 + 2.0 * d * (hq - hp) - 2.0 * d * d
    out = np.maximum(m, diag)
    if U > -1e299:
        out = np.maximum(out, U)
    return out


def try_pair_update(h, A, B, p, q, spans, curr, coarse_k, near_k, refine, n):
    hp = float(h[p])
    hq = float(h[q])
    lo = max(-hp, hq - 1.0)
    hi = min(1.0 - hp, hq)
    if hi - lo <= 1e-13:
        return False, 0.0, curr

    L = int(max(spans[p], spans[q]))
    half = L - 1
    c0 = float(A[0])

    if half > 0:
        ba = np.zeros(half)
        bb = np.zeros(half)

        a = min(half, n - 1 - p)
        if a > 0:
            ba[:a] += 1.0 - h[p + 1:p + 1 + a]
        a = min(half, p)
        if a > 0:
            ba[:a] -= h[p - 1:p - 1 - a:-1]
        a = min(half, p)
        if a > 0:
            bb[:a] += 1.0 - h[p - 1:p - 1 - a:-1]
        a = min(half, n - 1 - p)
        if a > 0:
            bb[:a] -= h[p + 1:p + 1 + a]

        a = min(half, n - 1 - q)
        if a > 0:
            ba[:a] -= 1.0 - h[q + 1:q + 1 + a]
        a = min(half, q)
        if a > 0:
            ba[:a] += h[q - 1:q - 1 - a:-1]
        a = min(half, q)
        if a > 0:
            bb[:a] -= 1.0 - h[q - 1:q - 1 - a:-1]
        a = min(half, n - 1 - q)
        if a > 0:
            bb[:a] += h[q + 1:q + 1 + a]

        v0 = np.concatenate((A[1:L], B[1:L]))
        bv = np.concatenate((ba, bb))
    else:
        ba = np.zeros(0)
        bb = np.zeros(0)
        v0 = np.zeros(0)
        bv = np.zeros(0)

    dist = abs(p - q)
    U = -1e300
    if L < n:
        U = max(float(A[L:].max()), float(B[L:].max()))

    d = build_d_grid(lo, hi, coarse_k, near_k)
    f = eval_pair(d, v0, bv, c0, half, dist, L, U, hp, hq)
    im = int(np.argmin(f))
    fb = float(f[im])
    db = float(d[im])

    if refine > 0 and db > lo + 1e-12 and db < hi - 1e-12:
        step = max((hi - lo) / float(max(1, coarse_k)), 1e-6)
        hlf = step
        for _ in range(refine):
            l = max(lo, db - hlf)
            r = min(hi, db + hlf)
            if r - l < 1e-14:
                break
            df = build_refine_grid(l, r, db, 23)
            ff = eval_pair(df, v0, bv, c0, half, dist, L, U, hp, hq)
            im2 = int(np.argmin(ff))
            f2 = float(ff[im2])
            d2 = float(df[im2])
            if f2 < fb - 1e-12:
                fb = f2
                db = d2
                if l + 1e-12 < db < r - 1e-12:
                    hlf *= 0.45
                else:
                    hlf *= 0.75
            else:
                break

    if abs(db) < 1e-13:
        return False, 0.0, curr

    chk = eval_pair(np.array([0.0, db]), v0, bv, c0, half, dist, L, U, hp, hq)
    if chk[1] < curr - 1e-8:
        h[p] = float(np.clip(hp + db, 0.0, 1.0))
        h[q] = float(np.clip(hq - db, 0.0, 1.0))
        if half > 0:
            A[1:L] += db * ba
            B[1:L] += db * bb
            if 0 < dist < L:
                A[dist] += db * db
                B[dist] += db * db
        nc0 = c0 + 2.0 * db * (hq - hp) - 2.0 * db * db
        if nc0 < 0.0:
            nc0 = 0.0
        A[0] = nc0
        B[0] = nc0
        return True, db, float(chk[1])
    return False, 0.0, curr


def pair_cd(h, n, deadline, spacing=1, coarse_k=27, near_k=9, refine=2, seed=1, max_passes=10000):
    h = project_feasible(h, n).copy()
    A, B = compute_ab(h, n)
    cmax = max(float(A.max()), float(B.max()))
    best_cmax = cmax
    best_h = h.copy()
    spans = get_spans(n)
    improved_any = False
    pass_i = 0

    while pass_i < max_passes and time.time() < deadline:
        A, B = compute_ab(h, n)
        cmax = max(float(A.max()), float(B.max()))
        improved = False
        if spacing >= n:
            break
        order = np.arange(0, n - spacing, spacing, dtype=int)
        if order.size:
            rng = np.random.RandomState(seed + pass_i)
            rng.shuffle(order)
        updated = 0
        for p in order:
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break
            q = p + spacing
            ok, _, fnew = try_pair_update(h, A, B, p, q, spans, cmax, coarse_k, near_k, refine, n)
            if ok:
                cmax = fnew
                improved = True
                updated += 1
                if updated % 25 == 0:
                    cmax = max(float(A.max()), float(B.max()))
                    if cmax > best_cmax + 1e-7:
                        h = best_h.copy()
                        A, B = compute_ab(h, n)
                        cmax = best_cmax
                        break

        if improved:
            improved_any = True
            A, B = compute_ab(h, n)
            cmax = max(float(A.max()), float(B.max()))
            if cmax < best_cmax - 1e-12:
                best_cmax = cmax
                best_h = h.copy()
            elif cmax > best_cmax + 1e-7:
                h = best_h.copy()
        else:
            break
        pass_i += 1

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    A, B = compute_ab(best_h, n)
    c5 = max(float(A.max()), float(B.max())) * (2.0 / n)
    return best_h, c5, improved_any


def random_pair_round(h, n, deadline, max_pairs, seed, spacing=0, coarse_k=19, near_k=6, refine=1):
    h = project_feasible(h, n).copy()
    A, B = compute_ab(h, n)
    cmax = max(float(A.max()), float(B.max()))
    best_cmax = cmax
    best_h = h.copy()
    spans = get_spans(n)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    improved_any = False
    stall = 0
    max_stall = max(250, max_pairs // 3)

    for i in range(max_pairs):
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        if spacing and spacing < n:
            p = int(rng.randint(0, n - spacing))
            q = p + spacing
        else:
            p = int(rng.randint(n))
            q = int(rng.randint(n))
            if p == q:
                continue
        ok, _, fnew = try_pair_update(h, A, B, p, q, spans, cmax, coarse_k, near_k, refine, n)
        if ok:
            improved_any = True
            cmax = fnew
            stall = 0
            if i % 25 == 0:
                cmax = max(float(A.max()), float(B.max()))
                if cmax < best_cmax - 1e-12:
                    best_cmax = cmax
                    best_h = h.copy()
                elif cmax > best_cmax + 1e-7:
                    h = best_h.copy()
                    A, B = compute_ab(h, n)
                    cmax = best_cmax
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > max_stall:
                break

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    A, B = compute_ab(best_h, n)
    c5 = max(float(A.max()), float(B.max())) * (2.0 / n)
    return best_h, c5, improved_any


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 10.0
    if deadline < t0 + 5.0:
        deadline = t0 + max(1.0, float(budget_s))

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
        h0 = None
        n0 = 0
    if h0 is None or n0 < 8:
        n0 = 700
        h0 = make_random_h(n0, seed)

    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    A0, B0 = compute_ab(h0, n0)
    best_c = max(float(A0.max()), float(B0.max())) * (2.0 / n0)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_n = n0

    if time.time() > deadline - 20.0:
        return best_h, c5_full(best_h, best_n), best_n

    cyc_seed = seed
    main_end = min(deadline, t0 + 520.0)
    while time.time() < main_end:
        cyc_improved = False
        for sp in (1, 2, 3, 1, 4, 6):
            if time.time() >= main_end:
                break
            if sp >= best_n:
                continue
            end = min(main_end, time.time() + 75.0)
            if end <= time.time() + 0.5:
                continue
            h2, c2, _ = pair_cd(best_h, best_n, end, sp, 25, 8, 2, cyc_seed)
            cyc_seed += 1
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_h = h2
                best_c = c2
                cyc_improved = True

        if time.time() >= main_end:
            break
        end = min(main_end, time.time() + 50.0)
        h2, c2, _ = random_pair_round(best_h, best_n, end, 3000, cyc_seed, 0, 21, 6, 1)
        cyc_seed += 101
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_h = h2
            best_c = c2
            cyc_improved = True
        if not cyc_improved:
            break

    candidates = [500, best_n, 650, 800, 1000, 450, 550, 750, 900, 1200]
    seen = set()
    for nt in candidates:
        if time.time() >= deadline - 70.0:
            break
        if nt in seen or nt < 20 or nt > 1400:
            continue
        seen.add(int(nt))
        if nt == best_n:
            h_try = best_h.copy()
        else:
            h_try = resample_h(best_h, best_n, int(nt))
        end = min(deadline - 60.0, time.time() + 70.0)
        if end <= time.time() + 0.5:
            continue
        h2, c2, _ = pair_cd(h_try, int(nt), end, 1, 25, 8, 2, cyc_seed)
        cyc_seed += 7
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-12 or (c2 <= best_c + 1e-12 and int(nt) < best_n):
            best_h = h2
            best_c = c2
            best_n = int(nt)
        if time.time() < deadline - 70.0:
            end = min(deadline - 60.0, time.time() + 35.0)
            h2, c2, _ = random_pair_round(best_h, best_n, end, 1200, cyc_seed, 0, 19, 5, 1)
            cyc_seed += 7
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-12 or (c2 <= best_c + 1e-12 and best_n < best_n):
                best_h = h2
                best_c = c2

    rst = cyc_seed
    no_impr = 0
    while time.time() < deadline - 25.0 and no_impr < 5:
        scale = 0.015 + 0.035 * (0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(float(rst)))
        h_try = perturb_h(best_h, best_n, rst, scale)
        end = min(deadline - 18.0, time.time() + 35.0)
        if end <= time.time() + 0.5:
            break
        h2, c2, _ = pair_cd(h_try, best_n, end, 1, 23, 8, 1, rst)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_h = h2
            best_c = c2
            no_impr = 0
        else:
            no_impr += 1
        if rst % 4 == 0 and time.time() < deadline - 30.0:
            end = min(deadline - 25.0, time.time() + 25.0)
            h2, c2, _ = random_pair_round(best_h, best_n, end, 800, rst + 991, 0, 17, 5, 1)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_h = h2
                best_c = c2
                no_impr = 0
        rst += 1

    if time.time() < deadline - 8.0:
        end = deadline - 3.0
        h2, c2, _ = pair_cd(best_h, best_n, end, 1, 29, 10, 3, rst)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_h = h2
            best_c = c2
        if time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
            h2, c2, _ = pair_cd(best_h, best_n, deadline - 1.0, 2, 25, 9, 1, rst + 13)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_h = h2
                best_c = c2

    s = float(best_h.sum())
    if abs(s - 0.5 * best_n) > 1e-11 or not np.all((best_h >= -1e-15) & (best_h <= 1.0 + 1e-15)):
        best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)

    best_h = np.clip(best_h, 0.0, 1.0)
    final_c = c5_full(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, float(final_c), best_n
```
